========== FILE: backup_before_batch1112\executive_dashboard.py ==========

"""Executive overview: all metrics, trends and exports use the SAME filtered rows."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import streamlit as st

from business_metrics import apply_dashboard_filters, revenue_by_period
from commerce_engine import NONE, calculate_commercial_metrics
from audit_exports import build_financial_export, excel_safe_csv
from performance_insights import category_profitability, loss_making_rows
from executive_addons import render_comparison_panel, render_readiness_panel
from chart_builder import render_chart_studio, render_performance_lab


def _default(options: list[str], preferred: list[str], fallback: int = 0) -> int:
    lower = {name.casefold(): i for i, name in enumerate(options)}
    for name in preferred:
        if name.casefold() in lower:
            return lower[name.casefold()]
    return fallback


def render_executive_dashboard(data: pd.DataFrame, dataset_name: str) -> None:
    st.header("Executive Performance Dashboard")
    st.caption(f"Dataset: {dataset_name}  •  All figures on this page reflect the selected filters.")
    st.caption("Want to change the graph type? Expand Section 07 · Interactive chart studio below; choose bar, line, area, donut, scatter or histogram.")
    if data.empty:
        st.warning("The active dataset has no records.")
        return

    numeric = data.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
    if not numeric:
        st.warning("No numeric financial fields are available. Prepare the source data first.")
        return

    text_fields = data.select_dtypes(include=["object", "string", "category"]).columns.tolist()
    category_options = ["No category filter"] + text_fields
    date_options = ["No date filter"] + [
        col for col in data.columns
        if "date" in str(col).casefold() or "time" in str(col).casefold()
        or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(data[col])
    ]

    st.markdown("### Business performance")
    with st.expander("01  ·  Refine dataset", expanded=True):
        first, second = st.columns(2)
        with first:
            category = st.selectbox(
                "Category", category_options,
                index=_default(category_options, ["Product", "Region", "City"]),
                key="ex_category",
            )
            selected = None
            category_values = []
            if category != "No category filter":
                category_values = sorted(data[category].dropna().astype(str).unique().tolist())
                if len(category_values) > 250:
                    st.info("This field has over 250 values; use another category for an interactive selector.")
                    category = "No category filter"
                else:
                    selected = st.multiselect(
                        "Values to include", category_values, default=category_values,
                        key=f"ex_values_{category}",
                    )
        with second:
            date_col = st.selectbox(
                "Date field", date_options,
                index=_default(date_options, ["Date", "Order Date", "Transaction Date"]),
                key="ex_date_field",
            )
            date_bounds = None
            if date_col != "No date filter":
                apply_date_range = st.checkbox(
                    "Restrict records to a date range", value=False, key="ex_use_date_filter"
                )
                parsed = pd.to_datetime(data[date_col], errors="coerce", format="mixed").dropna()
                if not parsed.empty and apply_date_range:
                    date_bounds = st.date_input(
                        "Date range", value=(parsed.min().date(), parsed.max().date()),
                        min_value=parsed.min().date(), max_value=parsed.max().date(),
                        key=f"ex_date_bounds_{date_col}",
                    )
                elif parsed.empty:
                    st.warning("No valid dates are available in the selected field.")
        numeric_filter = st.selectbox(
            "Additional numeric filter", ["No numeric filter"] + numeric,
            key="ex_number_field",
        )
        value_bounds = None
        if numeric_filter != "No numeric filter":
            series = pd.to_numeric(data[numeric_filter], errors="coerce").dropna()
            series = series[series.map(lambda v: bool(pd.notna(v)) and abs(v) != float("inf"))]
            if not series.empty:
                lower_default, upper_default = float(series.min()), float(series.max())
                n1, n2 = st.columns(2)
                with n1:
                    low = st.number_input("Minimum", value=lower_default, key=f"ex_min_{numeric_filter}")
                with n2:
                    high = st.number_input("Maximum", value=upper_default, key=f"ex_max_{numeric_filter}")
                value_bounds = (low, high)

    # A partially selected date range must not silently apply only one endpoint.
    start = end = None
    if date_col != "No date filter" and isinstance(date_bounds, (list, tuple)):
        if len(date_bounds) == 2:
            start, end = date_bounds
        else:
            st.info("Choose both endpoints to apply the date filter.")
    if value_bounds is not None and value_bounds[0] > value_bounds[1]:
        st.error("Minimum must be less than or equal to maximum.")
        return

    try:
        filtered = apply_dashboard_filters(
            data,
            # All selected means unrestricted, including source rows with missing categories.
            category_column=category if selected is not None and len(selected) < len(category_values) else None,
            categories=selected if selected is not None and len(selected) < len(category_values) else None,
            date_column=date_col if start is not None else None,
            start_date=start, end_date=end,
            numeric_column=numeric_filter if value_bounds is not None else None,
            numeric_min=value_bounds[0] if value_bounds is not None else None,
            numeric_max=value_bounds[1] if value_bounds is not None else None,
        ).reset_index(drop=True)
    except (ValueError, TypeError) as error:
        st.error(f"The filter is invalid: {error}")
        return

    st.caption(f"{len(filtered):,} of {len(data):,} records matched the filters.")
    if filtered.empty:
        st.warning("No records match. Adjust the filters to continue.")
        return

    with st.expander("02  ·  Confirm financial field mapping", expanded=True):
        a, b = st.columns(2)
        with a:
            revenue_col = st.selectbox(
                "Gross revenue field", numeric,
                index=_default(numeric, ["Sales", "Gross Revenue", "Revenue"]),
                key="ex_revenue",
            )
            cost_col = st.selectbox(
                "Cost of sales field (optional)", [NONE] + numeric,
                index=_default([NONE] + numeric, ["Total Cost", "COGS", "Cost"]),
                key="ex_cost",
            )
        with b:
            discount_col = st.selectbox("Discounts (optional)", [NONE] + numeric, key="ex_discount")
            returns_col = st.selectbox("Returns (optional)", [NONE] + numeric, key="ex_returns")
        st.caption(
            "Gross revenue must be before separately mapped discounts/returns. "
            "Do not map a field twice or treat Profit as a cost. All amounts must use one currency."
        )

    try:
        calculations, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(
            filtered, revenue_col, cost_col, discount_col, returns_col
        )
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as error:
        st.error(f"Financial calculation blocked: {error}")
        return

    headline = st.columns(4)
    headline[0].metric("Matching Records", f"{len(filtered):,}")
    headline[1].metric("Gross Revenue", f"{totals['gross_revenue']:,.2f}")
    headline[2].metric("Net Revenue", f"{totals['net_revenue']:,.2f}")
    if "profit_after_mapped_costs" in totals:
        headline[3].metric("Profit after mapped costs", f"{totals['profit_after_mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        sub = st.columns(3)
        sub[0].metric("Mapped costs", f"{totals['mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        sub[1].metric("Net business loss", f"{totals['net_business_loss']:,.2f}")
        margin = totals["profit_margin_pct"]
        sub[2].metric("Profit margin", f"{margin:.2f}%" if margin is not None else "Not defined")
        st.caption("Profit shown here is after mapped costs; it is not labelled net profit unless all relevant costs are established.")
    else:
        headline[3].metric("Profit", "Not available")
        st.info("Map a cost-of-sales field to enable profit, loss and margin metrics.")

    chart_col, chart_col2 = st.columns(2)
    with chart_col:
        st.markdown("#### Revenue by category")
        if category != "No category filter" or text_fields:
            dimension = category if category != "No category filter" else text_fields[0]
            chart_frame = pd.DataFrame({
                "Category": filtered[dimension].astype("string").fillna("(Missing)").to_numpy(),
                "Net Revenue": calculations["Net Revenue"].to_numpy(),
            })
            category_summary = (
                chart_frame.groupby("Category", as_index=False, dropna=False)["Net Revenue"]
                .sum().sort_values("Net Revenue", ascending=False).head(12)
            )
            st.plotly_chart(
                px.bar(category_summary, x="Category", y="Net Revenue", title="Top 12 categories"),
                width="stretch",
            )
        else:
            st.info("Add a text/category field to compare revenue across groups.")
    with chart_col2:
        st.markdown("#### Revenue trend")
        if date_col == "No date filter":
            st.info("Select a date field above to display period trends.")
        else:
            cadence = st.selectbox("Period", ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"], key="ex_cadence")
            try:
                time_series, excluded_dates = revenue_by_period(
                    filtered[date_col], calculations["Net Revenue"], cadence
                )
                if excluded_dates:
                    st.warning(f"{excluded_dates:,} rows with invalid/missing dates were excluded from this trend only.")
                if not time_series.empty:
                    st.plotly_chart(
                        px.line(time_series, x="Period", y="Revenue", markers=True,
                                title="Net revenue by period"), width="stretch",
                    )
                    if time_series["Growth %"].notna().any():
                        st.caption("Growth is calculated only for adjacent periods with positive prior-period revenue.")
                else:
                    st.info("No valid dated records remain after filtering.")
            except ValueError as error:
                st.warning(f"Cannot calculate the trend: {error}")

    with st.expander("03  ·  Audit records and download", expanded=False):
        st.dataframe(filtered.head(200), width="stretch")
        output = build_financial_export(filtered, calculations)
        st.download_button(
            "Download filtered records and financial calculations",
            data=excel_safe_csv(output),
            file_name="python_bi_executive_filtered_data.csv", mime="text/csv", key="ex_export",
        )
        st.caption(
            "Export includes all matching records, not just the 200 previewed rows. "
            "Calculated fields are named BI_Gross_Revenue, BI_Net_Revenue, etc. "
            "Spreadsheet-dangerous text is protected in the exported copy."
        )

    if "Mapped Costs" in calculations.columns:
        with st.expander("04  ·  Category profitability and transaction exceptions", expanded=False):
            st.caption(
                "These results use exactly the filtered records and financial field mapping above. "
                "Profit is after mapped costs; it is not necessarily accounting net profit."
            )
            available_categories = [
                c for c in filtered.columns
                if pd.api.types.is_object_dtype(filtered[c])
                or pd.api.types.is_string_dtype(filtered[c])
                or isinstance(filtered[c].dtype, pd.CategoricalDtype)
            ]
            if available_categories:
                category_field = st.selectbox(
                    "Group profitability by", available_categories,
                    index=_default(available_categories, ["Product", "Region", "City"]),
                    key="ex_profit_category",
                )
                groups = category_profitability(filtered, calculations, category_field)
                st.dataframe(groups.head(100), width="stretch", hide_index=True)
                st.download_button(
                    "Export category profitability",
                    data=excel_safe_csv(groups),
                    file_name="python_bi_category_profitability.csv",
                    mime="text/csv", key="ex_profitability_export",
                )
                st.caption(
                    "Category totals reconcile to the filtered net revenue, mapped costs "
                    "and aggregate profit above. A zero net-revenue category has undefined margin."
                )
            else:
                st.info("No categorical source field is available for grouped analysis.")
            exceptions = loss_making_rows(filtered, calculations)
            st.metric("Loss-making transactions", f"{len(exceptions):,}")
            if not exceptions.empty:
                st.dataframe(exceptions.head(100), width="stretch")
                st.download_button(
                    "Export loss-making transactions",
                    data=excel_safe_csv(exceptions),
                    file_name="python_bi_loss_making_transactions.csv",
                    mime="text/csv", key="ex_loss_export",
                )
            else:
                st.success("No transactions have a negative profit after mapped costs.")

    # Phase 3 Batches 7 + 8: optional consolidated analytical diagnostics.
    render_comparison_panel(filtered, calculations)
    render_readiness_panel(data)

    # Phase 3 Batches 9 + 10: chart selection without leaving Executive Overview.
    render_chart_studio(filtered, calculations)
    # Intentionally source-wide and opt-in, unlike Dashboard-filtered charts.
    render_performance_lab(data)



FOLDER: backup_before_batch1314

========== FILE: backup_before_batch1314\executive_dashboard.py ==========

"""Executive overview: all metrics, trends and exports use the SAME filtered rows."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import streamlit as st

from business_metrics import apply_dashboard_filters, revenue_by_period
from commerce_engine import NONE, calculate_commercial_metrics
from audit_exports import build_financial_export, excel_safe_csv
from performance_insights import category_profitability, loss_making_rows
from executive_addons import render_comparison_panel, render_readiness_panel
from chart_builder import render_chart_studio, render_performance_lab
from executive_report import render_executive_report


def _default(options: list[str], preferred: list[str], fallback: int = 0) -> int:
    lower = {name.casefold(): i for i, name in enumerate(options)}
    for name in preferred:
        if name.casefold() in lower:
            return lower[name.casefold()]
    return fallback


def render_executive_dashboard(data: pd.DataFrame, dataset_name: str) -> None:
    st.header("Executive Performance Dashboard")
    st.caption(f"Dataset: {dataset_name}  •  All figures on this page reflect the selected filters.")
    st.caption("Want to change the graph type? Expand Section 07 · Interactive chart studio below; choose bar, line, area, donut, scatter or histogram.")
    if data.empty:
        st.warning("The active dataset has no records.")
        return

    numeric = data.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
    if not numeric:
        st.warning("No numeric financial fields are available. Prepare the source data first.")
        return

    text_fields = data.select_dtypes(include=["object", "string", "category"]).columns.tolist()
    category_options = ["No category filter"] + text_fields
    date_options = ["No date filter"] + [
        col for col in data.columns
        if "date" in str(col).casefold() or "time" in str(col).casefold()
        or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(data[col])
    ]

    st.markdown("### Business performance")
    with st.expander("01  ·  Refine dataset", expanded=True):
        first, second = st.columns(2)
        with first:
            category = st.selectbox(
                "Category", category_options,
                index=_default(category_options, ["Product", "Region", "City"]),
                key="ex_category",
            )
            selected = None
            category_values = []
            if category != "No category filter":
                category_values = sorted(data[category].dropna().astype(str).unique().tolist())
                if len(category_values) > 250:
                    st.info("This field has over 250 values; use another category for an interactive selector.")
                    category = "No category filter"
                else:
                    selected = st.multiselect(
                        "Values to include", category_values, default=category_values,
                        key=f"ex_values_{category}",
                    )
        with second:
            date_col = st.selectbox(
                "Date field", date_options,
                index=_default(date_options, ["Date", "Order Date", "Transaction Date"]),
                key="ex_date_field",
            )
            date_bounds = None
            if date_col != "No date filter":
                apply_date_range = st.checkbox(
                    "Restrict records to a date range", value=False, key="ex_use_date_filter"
                )
                parsed = pd.to_datetime(data[date_col], errors="coerce", format="mixed").dropna()
                if not parsed.empty and apply_date_range:
                    date_bounds = st.date_input(
                        "Date range", value=(parsed.min().date(), parsed.max().date()),
                        min_value=parsed.min().date(), max_value=parsed.max().date(),
                        key=f"ex_date_bounds_{date_col}",
                    )
                elif parsed.empty:
                    st.warning("No valid dates are available in the selected field.")
        numeric_filter = st.selectbox(
            "Additional numeric filter", ["No numeric filter"] + numeric,
            key="ex_number_field",
        )
        value_bounds = None
        if numeric_filter != "No numeric filter":
            series = pd.to_numeric(data[numeric_filter], errors="coerce").dropna()
            series = series[series.map(lambda v: bool(pd.notna(v)) and abs(v) != float("inf"))]
            if not series.empty:
                lower_default, upper_default = float(series.min()), float(series.max())
                n1, n2 = st.columns(2)
                with n1:
                    low = st.number_input("Minimum", value=lower_default, key=f"ex_min_{numeric_filter}")
                with n2:
                    high = st.number_input("Maximum", value=upper_default, key=f"ex_max_{numeric_filter}")
                value_bounds = (low, high)

    # A partially selected date range must not silently apply only one endpoint.
    start = end = None
    if date_col != "No date filter" and isinstance(date_bounds, (list, tuple)):
        if len(date_bounds) == 2:
            start, end = date_bounds
        else:
            st.info("Choose both endpoints to apply the date filter.")
    if value_bounds is not None and value_bounds[0] > value_bounds[1]:
        st.error("Minimum must be less than or equal to maximum.")
        return

    try:
        filtered = apply_dashboard_filters(
            data,
            # All selected means unrestricted, including source rows with missing categories.
            category_column=category if selected is not None and len(selected) < len(category_values) else None,
            categories=selected if selected is not None and len(selected) < len(category_values) else None,
            date_column=date_col if start is not None else None,
            start_date=start, end_date=end,
            numeric_column=numeric_filter if value_bounds is not None else None,
            numeric_min=value_bounds[0] if value_bounds is not None else None,
            numeric_max=value_bounds[1] if value_bounds is not None else None,
        ).reset_index(drop=True)
    except (ValueError, TypeError) as error:
        st.error(f"The filter is invalid: {error}")
        return

    st.caption(f"{len(filtered):,} of {len(data):,} records matched the filters.")
    if filtered.empty:
        st.warning("No records match. Adjust the filters to continue.")
        return

    with st.expander("02  ·  Confirm financial field mapping", expanded=True):
        a, b = st.columns(2)
        with a:
            revenue_col = st.selectbox(
                "Gross revenue field", numeric,
                index=_default(numeric, ["Sales", "Gross Revenue", "Revenue"]),
                key="ex_revenue",
            )
            cost_col = st.selectbox(
                "Cost of sales field (optional)", [NONE] + numeric,
                index=_default([NONE] + numeric, ["Total Cost", "COGS", "Cost"]),
                key="ex_cost",
            )
        with b:
            discount_col = st.selectbox("Discounts (optional)", [NONE] + numeric, key="ex_discount")
            returns_col = st.selectbox("Returns (optional)", [NONE] + numeric, key="ex_returns")
        st.caption(
            "Gross revenue must be before separately mapped discounts/returns. "
            "Do not map a field twice or treat Profit as a cost. All amounts must use one currency."
        )

    try:
        calculations, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(
            filtered, revenue_col, cost_col, discount_col, returns_col
        )
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as error:
        st.error(f"Financial calculation blocked: {error}")
        return

    headline = st.columns(4)
    headline[0].metric("Matching Records", f"{len(filtered):,}")
    headline[1].metric("Gross Revenue", f"{totals['gross_revenue']:,.2f}")
    headline[2].metric("Net Revenue", f"{totals['net_revenue']:,.2f}")
    if "profit_after_mapped_costs" in totals:
        headline[3].metric("Profit after mapped costs", f"{totals['profit_after_mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        sub = st.columns(3)
        sub[0].metric("Mapped costs", f"{totals['mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        sub[1].metric("Net business loss", f"{totals['net_business_loss']:,.2f}")
        margin = totals["profit_margin_pct"]
        sub[2].metric("Profit margin", f"{margin:.2f}%" if margin is not None else "Not defined")
        st.caption("Profit shown here is after mapped costs; it is not labelled net profit unless all relevant costs are established.")
    else:
        headline[3].metric("Profit", "Not available")
        st.info("Map a cost-of-sales field to enable profit, loss and margin metrics.")

    chart_col, chart_col2 = st.columns(2)
    with chart_col:
        st.markdown("#### Revenue by category")
        if category != "No category filter" or text_fields:
            dimension = category if category != "No category filter" else text_fields[0]
            chart_frame = pd.DataFrame({
                "Category": filtered[dimension].astype("string").fillna("(Missing)").to_numpy(),
                "Net Revenue": calculations["Net Revenue"].to_numpy(),
            })
            category_summary = (
                chart_frame.groupby("Category", as_index=False, dropna=False)["Net Revenue"]
                .sum().sort_values("Net Revenue", ascending=False).head(12)
            )
            st.plotly_chart(
                px.bar(category_summary, x="Category", y="Net Revenue", title="Top 12 categories"),
                width="stretch",
            )
        else:
            st.info("Add a text/category field to compare revenue across groups.")
    with chart_col2:
        st.markdown("#### Revenue trend")
        if date_col == "No date filter":
            st.info("Select a date field above to display period trends.")
        else:
            cadence = st.selectbox("Period", ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"], key="ex_cadence")
            try:
                time_series, excluded_dates = revenue_by_period(
                    filtered[date_col], calculations["Net Revenue"], cadence
                )
                if excluded_dates:
                    st.warning(f"{excluded_dates:,} rows with invalid/missing dates were excluded from this trend only.")
                if not time_series.empty:
                    st.plotly_chart(
                        px.line(time_series, x="Period", y="Revenue", markers=True,
                                title="Net revenue by period"), width="stretch",
                    )
                    if time_series["Growth %"].notna().any():
                        st.caption("Growth is calculated only for adjacent periods with positive prior-period revenue.")
                else:
                    st.info("No valid dated records remain after filtering.")
            except ValueError as error:
                st.warning(f"Cannot calculate the trend: {error}")

    with st.expander("03  ·  Audit records and download", expanded=False):
        st.dataframe(filtered.head(200), width="stretch")
        output = build_financial_export(filtered, calculations)
        st.download_button(
            "Download filtered records and financial calculations",
            data=excel_safe_csv(output),
            file_name="python_bi_executive_filtered_data.csv", mime="text/csv", key="ex_export",
        )
        st.caption(
            "Export includes all matching records, not just the 200 previewed rows. "
            "Calculated fields are named BI_Gross_Revenue, BI_Net_Revenue, etc. "
            "Spreadsheet-dangerous text is protected in the exported copy."
        )

    if "Mapped Costs" in calculations.columns:
        with st.expander("04  ·  Category profitability and transaction exceptions", expanded=False):
            st.caption(
                "These results use exactly the filtered records and financial field mapping above. "
                "Profit is after mapped costs; it is not necessarily accounting net profit."
            )
            available_categories = [
                c for c in filtered.columns
                if pd.api.types.is_object_dtype(filtered[c])
                or pd.api.types.is_string_dtype(filtered[c])
                or isinstance(filtered[c].dtype, pd.CategoricalDtype)
            ]
            if available_categories:
                category_field = st.selectbox(
                    "Group profitability by", available_categories,
                    index=_default(available_categories, ["Product", "Region", "City"]),
                    key="ex_profit_category",
                )
                groups = category_profitability(filtered, calculations, category_field)
                st.dataframe(groups.head(100), width="stretch", hide_index=True)
                st.download_button(
                    "Export category profitability",
                    data=excel_safe_csv(groups),
                    file_name="python_bi_category_profitability.csv",
                    mime="text/csv", key="ex_profitability_export",
                )
                st.caption(
                    "Category totals reconcile to the filtered net revenue, mapped costs "
                    "and aggregate profit above. A zero net-revenue category has undefined margin."
                )
            else:
                st.info("No categorical source field is available for grouped analysis.")
            exceptions = loss_making_rows(filtered, calculations)
            st.metric("Loss-making transactions", f"{len(exceptions):,}")
            if not exceptions.empty:
                st.dataframe(exceptions.head(100), width="stretch")
                st.download_button(
                    "Export loss-making transactions",
                    data=excel_safe_csv(exceptions),
                    file_name="python_bi_loss_making_transactions.csv",
                    mime="text/csv", key="ex_loss_export",
                )
            else:
                st.success("No transactions have a negative profit after mapped costs.")

    # Phase 3 Batches 7 + 8: optional consolidated analytical diagnostics.
    render_comparison_panel(filtered, calculations)
    render_readiness_panel(data)

    # Phase 3 Batches 9 + 10: chart selection without leaving Executive Overview.
    render_chart_studio(filtered, calculations)
    # Intentionally source-wide and opt-in, unlike Dashboard-filtered charts.
    render_performance_lab(data)

    # Phase 3 Batches 11 + 12 — executive report uses the same filtered results.
    filter_descriptions = [f"{len(filtered):,} of {len(data):,} source records included"]
    if category != "No category filter":
        filter_descriptions.append(f"Category: {category}")
        if selected is not None:
            filter_descriptions.append(f"Selected values: {', '.join(map(str, selected[:20]))}" +
                                       (" (additional values omitted from label)" if len(selected) > 20 else ""))
    if start is not None:
        filter_descriptions.append(f"Dates {start} through {end} in {date_col}")
    if value_bounds is not None:
        filter_descriptions.append(f"{numeric_filter} from {value_bounds[0]:,.2f} to {value_bounds[1]:,.2f}")
    mapping = {"Gross revenue": revenue_col, "Cost of sales": cost_col,
               "Discounts": discount_col, "Returns": returns_col}
    render_executive_report(
        filtered, calculations, totals,
        dataset_name=dataset_name,
        mapping=mapping,
        filter_summary="; ".join(filter_descriptions),
    )

"""Streamlit Section 09 — on-demand, audited executive report downloads."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import streamlit as st

from audit_exports import excel_safe_csv
from report_engine import build_executive_report


def render_executive_report(
    filtered: pd.DataFrame,
    calculations: pd.DataFrame,
    totals: dict,
    *,
    dataset_name: str,
    mapping: dict[str, str],
    filter_summary: str,
) -> None:
    with st.expander("09  ·  Executive report centre", expanded=False):
        st.caption(
            "Create a standalone, print-ready executive HTML report from the same filtered records "
            "and commercial mapping used above. CSV reports include all category/period rows. "
            "No transactions or files leave your computer."
        )
        categories = [c for c in filtered.columns if
                      pd.api.types.is_object_dtype(filtered[c])
                      or pd.api.types.is_string_dtype(filtered[c])
                      or isinstance(filtered[c].dtype, pd.CategoricalDtype)]
        dates = [c for c in filtered.columns if
                 "date" in str(c).casefold() or "time" in str(c).casefold()
                 or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(filtered[c])]
        a, b = st.columns(2)
        with a:
            group = st.selectbox(
                "Report category", ["No category breakdown"] + categories,
                index=(["No category breakdown"] + categories).index("Product")
                if "Product" in categories else 0, key="exec_report_group",
            )
            frequency = st.selectbox(
                "Report calendar periods", ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"],
                key="exec_report_frequency",
            )
        with b:
            date = st.selectbox(
                "Report date field", ["No time breakdown"] + dates,
                index=(["No time breakdown"] + dates).index("Date")
                if "Date" in dates else 0, key="exec_report_date",
            )
            unit = st.text_input(
                "Currency / reporting unit", value="Not specified",
                max_chars=40, help="A label only; figures are not converted between currencies.",
                key="exec_report_unit",
            )
        st.caption(
            "The printed report shows aggregate results only, not customer-level transaction records. "
            "The transaction-level CSV audit remains in Section 03. "
            "Use Chrome/Edge Print → Save as PDF after opening the downloaded HTML file."
        )
        if not st.button("Generate executive report", key="exec_report_generate", type="primary"):
            return
        try:
            bundle = build_executive_report(
                filtered, calculations, totals,
                dataset_name=dataset_name,
                category=None if group == "No category breakdown" else group,
                date_column=None if date == "No time breakdown" else date,
                period=frequency,
                field_mapping=mapping,
                filter_notes=filter_summary,
                currency_label=unit.strip() or "Not specified",
            )
        except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as exc:
            st.error(f"Report generation stopped: {exc}")
            return
        st.success(f"Report ready for {bundle['matching_rows']:,} filtered transactions.")
        st.download_button(
            "Download executive report (HTML)", data=bundle["html"],
            file_name="python_bi_executive_report.html", mime="text/html",
            key="exec_report_html_download", on_click="ignore",
        )
        left, middle, right = st.columns(3)
        with left:
            st.download_button(
                "Download KPI summary (CSV)", excel_safe_csv(bundle["summary"]),
                file_name="python_bi_report_kpis.csv", mime="text/csv",
                key="exec_report_kpi_csv", on_click="ignore",
            )
        with middle:
            if not bundle["categories"].empty:
                st.download_button(
                    "Download category report (CSV)", excel_safe_csv(bundle["categories"]),
                    file_name="python_bi_report_categories.csv", mime="text/csv",
                    key="exec_report_category_csv", on_click="ignore",
                )
        with right:
            if not bundle["periods"].empty:
                st.download_button(
                    "Download period report (CSV)", excel_safe_csv(bundle["periods"]),
                    file_name="python_bi_report_periods.csv", mime="text/csv",
                    key="exec_report_period_csv", on_click="ignore",
                )
        st.dataframe(bundle["summary"], width="stretch", hide_index=True)
        st.caption("Report totals are checked against the same underlying row-level commercial calculations.")

"""Pure tests for Batch 11 (audited report) and Batch 12 (Home integration).
Run: python test_phase3_batch1112.py
Real Streamlit page interaction must also be tested once in the browser.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
import math
import sys
import types

import pandas as pd

# This calculation logic can run even where the optional Streamlit UI is not installed.
try:
    import streamlit  # noqa: F401
except ImportError:
    sys.modules["streamlit"] = types.SimpleNamespace()

from commerce_engine import calculate_commercial_metrics
from report_engine import build_executive_report


def failed(function):
    try:
        function()
    except ValueError:
        return
    raise AssertionError("Expected a ValueError")


def close(a, b):
    assert math.isclose(float(a), float(b), rel_tol=1e-10, abs_tol=.01), (a, b)


def test_from_sample_workbook():
    workbook = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "Data" / "Python_BI_Complete_Business_Dataset.xlsx"
    if not workbook.exists():
        print("Bundled workbook not present; pure tests continue.")
        return
    source = pd.read_excel(workbook, sheet_name="Business Data")
    original = source.copy(deep=True)
    calc, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(source, "Sales", "Total Cost")
    bundle = build_executive_report(
        source, calc, totals,
        dataset_name="Python BI Business Transactions 2026", category="Product",
        date_column="Date", period="Monthly",
        field_mapping={"Gross revenue": "Sales", "Cost of sales": "Total Cost"},
        filter_notes="All source records", currency_label="Unspecified",
        generated_at=datetime(2026, 11, 7, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )
    close(totals["net_revenue"], 293300)
    close(totals["mapped_costs"], 231800)
    close(totals["profit_after_mapped_costs"], 61500)
    assert bundle["matching_rows"] == 20
    assert bundle["missing_date_rows"] == 0
    close(bundle["categories"]["Net Revenue"].sum(), 293300)
    close(bundle["categories"]["Profit After Mapped Costs"].sum(), 61500)
    assert bundle["periods"]["Period"].tolist() == ["2026-01"]
    close(bundle["periods"]["Revenue"].sum(), 293300)
    html = bundle["html"].decode("utf-8")
    assert "Business Performance Report" in html
    assert "01. Financial measures" in html and "04. Field mapping and assumptions" in html
    assert "2026-01" in html and "Save as PDF" in html
    assert "2026-11-07" not in html  # printed date deliberately human-readable, not raw string
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(source, original)
    print("Practice workbook: 20 records; revenue/cost/profit/category/time totals reconcile")


def test_malicious_text_and_missing_dates():
    source = pd.DataFrame({
        "Date": ["2026-01-01", "2026-02-01", "BAD"],
        "Product": ["Widget", "<script>alert('x')</script>", None],
        "Sales": [100.0, 210.0, 90.0],
        "Cost": [30.0, 300.0, 20.0],
    }, index=[4, 4, 9])
    before = source.copy(deep=True)
    calc, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(source, "Sales", "Cost")
    bundle = build_executive_report(
        source, calc, totals,
        dataset_name='<img src=x onerror="alert(1)">', category="Product",
        date_column="Date", period="Monthly",
        field_mapping={"Gross revenue": "Sales", "Injected key": "<b>unsafe</b>"},
        filter_notes='City = "<script>"',
        generated_at=datetime(2026, 11, 7, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )
    html = bundle["html"].decode("utf-8")
    assert "<script>alert" not in html and "&lt;script&gt;alert" in html
    assert '<img src=x onerror' not in html and '&lt;img src=x onerror' in html
    assert "<b>unsafe</b>" not in html and "&lt;b&gt;unsafe&lt;/b&gt;" in html
    assert "(Missing)" in html
    assert bundle["missing_date_rows"] == 1
    assert bundle["periods"]["Period"].tolist() == ["2026-01", "2026-02"]
    close(bundle["periods"]["Revenue"].sum(), 310)
    close(bundle["categories"]["Net Revenue"].sum(), 400)
    close(bundle["categories"]["Profit After Mapped Costs"].sum(), 50)
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(source, before)

    # Bad mapping / report totals / row-count mismatch must stop a report.
    defaults = dict(dataset_name="X", category="Product", date_column="Date", period="Monthly",
                    field_mapping={"Gross revenue":"Sales"}, filter_notes="All")
    failed(lambda: build_executive_report(source, calc.iloc[:2], totals, **defaults))
    failed(lambda: build_executive_report(source, calc, dict(totals, net_revenue=9), **defaults))
    failed(lambda: build_executive_report(source, calc, totals, **dict(defaults, period="Weekly")))
    failed(lambda: build_executive_report(source, calc, totals, **dict(defaults, category="Absent")))
    # Missing expenses: do NOT create pretend profit or margin.
    no_cost_calc, no_cost_totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(source, "Sales")
    no_cost = build_executive_report(source, no_cost_calc, no_cost_totals, **defaults)
    assert "Profit After Mapped Costs" not in no_cost["categories"].columns
    assert no_cost["summary"].loc[
        no_cost["summary"].Metric == "Profit After Mapped Costs", "Value"
    ].isna().all()
    assert "No cost field was mapped" in no_cost["html"].decode("utf-8")


def test_home_code_wiring():
    project = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    application = (project / "app.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    dashboard = (project / "executive_dashboard.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    style = (project / "bi_theme.css").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "from home_experience import render_home" in application
    assert 'if page == "Home":\n    render_home()' in application
    assert "from executive_report import render_executive_report" in dashboard
    assert "render_executive_report(" in dashboard
    assert ".bi-home-hero" in style and ".bi-home-grid" in style
    assert "render_chart_studio(filtered, calculations)" in dashboard
    assert "render_performance_lab(data)" in dashboard
    assert '"Data Workspace", "Dashboard"' in application
    # Existing Phase 2 persistence and Phase 3 modules are still in the app.
    assert "def restore_active_dataset" in application
    assert "def save_active_dataset" in application


def test_mocked_ui():
    # Dry-run new widgets without starting the full browser server.
    import executive_report
    import home_experience

    class Context:
        def __enter__(self):
            return self
        def __exit__(self, *_args):
            return False
        def metric(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            pass

    class FakeStreamlit:
        def __init__(self):
            self.session_state = {}
            self.downloads = []
            self.markups = []
            self.captions = []
        def expander(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            return Context()
        def columns(self, count):
            return [Context() for _ in range(count)]
        def selectbox(self, _label, choices, index=0, **_kwargs):
            return choices[index]
        def text_input(self, _label, value='', **_kwargs):
            return value
        def button(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            return True
        def caption(self, *args, **_kwargs):
            self.captions.extend(args)
        def info(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            pass
        def markdown(self, *args, **_kwargs):
            self.markups.extend(args)
        def metric(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            pass
        def dataframe(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            pass
        def success(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            pass
        def warning(self, *args, **_kwargs):
            raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected warning: {args}")
        def error(self, *args, **_kwargs):
            raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected error: {args}")
        def download_button(self, *args, **kwargs):
            assert kwargs.get('on_click') == 'ignore', 'Report download should not rerun Streamlit'
            self.downloads.append((args, kwargs))

    fake = FakeStreamlit()
    former_report = executive_report.st
    former_home = home_experience.st
    executive_report.st = fake
    home_experience.st = fake
    try:
        source = pd.DataFrame({
            'Date': ['2026-01-01', '2026-02-01'],
            'Product': ['A','B'], 'Sales': [150.0, 75.0], 'Cost': [50.0,80.0],
        })
        calc, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(source,'Sales','Cost')
        executive_report.render_executive_report(source,calc,totals,
                                                 dataset_name='Test', mapping={'Sales':'Sales'},
                                                 filter_summary='All')
        assert len(fake.downloads) == 4, 'Expected HTML and three CSV downloads'
        assert any(item[1].get('file_name') == 'python_bi_executive_report.html'
                   for item in fake.downloads)
        fake.session_state['uploaded_df'] = source
        fake.session_state['uploaded_name'] = '<Customer & Company>'
        home_experience.render_home()
        assert any('Clarity in every decision' in text for text in fake.markups)
        assert any('&lt;Customer &amp; Company&gt;' in text for text in fake.markups)
    finally:
        executive_report.st = former_report
        home_experience.st = former_home


if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_from_sample_workbook()
    test_malicious_text_and_missing_dates()
    test_home_code_wiring()
    test_mocked_ui()
    print("PASS: Report reconciliations, escaped HTML, missing dates, missing costs, invalid inputs, source immutability, application integration")

========== FILE: backup_before_batch56\executive_dashboard.py ==========

"""Executive overview: all metrics, trends and exports use the SAME filtered rows."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import streamlit as st

from business_metrics import apply_dashboard_filters, revenue_by_period
from commerce_engine import NONE, calculate_commercial_metrics


def _default(options: list[str], preferred: list[str], fallback: int = 0) -> int:
    lower = {name.casefold(): i for i, name in enumerate(options)}
    for name in preferred:
        if name.casefold() in lower:
            return lower[name.casefold()]
    return fallback


def render_executive_dashboard(data: pd.DataFrame, dataset_name: str) -> None:
    st.header("Executive Performance Dashboard")
    st.caption(f"Dataset: {dataset_name}  •  All figures on this page reflect the selected filters.")
    if data.empty:
        st.warning("The active dataset has no records.")
        return

    numeric = data.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
    if not numeric:
        st.warning("No numeric financial fields are available. Prepare the source data first.")
        return

    text_fields = data.select_dtypes(include=["object", "string", "category"]).columns.tolist()
    category_options = ["No category filter"] + text_fields
    date_options = ["No date filter"] + [
        col for col in data.columns
        if "date" in str(col).casefold() or "time" in str(col).casefold()
        or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(data[col])
    ]

    st.markdown("### Business performance")
    with st.expander("01  ·  Refine dataset", expanded=True):
        first, second = st.columns(2)
        with first:
            category = st.selectbox(
                "Category", category_options,
                index=_default(category_options, ["Product", "Region", "City"]),
                key="ex_category",
            )
            selected = None
            category_values = []
            if category != "No category filter":
                category_values = sorted(data[category].dropna().astype(str).unique().tolist())
                if len(category_values) > 250:
                    st.info("This field has over 250 values; use another category for an interactive selector.")
                    category = "No category filter"
                else:
                    selected = st.multiselect(
                        "Values to include", category_values, default=category_values,
                        key=f"ex_values_{category}",
                    )
        with second:
            date_col = st.selectbox(
                "Date field", date_options,
                index=_default(date_options, ["Date", "Order Date", "Transaction Date"]),
                key="ex_date_field",
            )
            date_bounds = None
            if date_col != "No date filter":
                apply_date_range = st.checkbox(
                    "Restrict records to a date range", value=False, key="ex_use_date_filter"
                )
                parsed = pd.to_datetime(data[date_col], errors="coerce", format="mixed").dropna()
                if not parsed.empty and apply_date_range:
                    date_bounds = st.date_input(
                        "Date range", value=(parsed.min().date(), parsed.max().date()),
                        min_value=parsed.min().date(), max_value=parsed.max().date(),
                        key=f"ex_date_bounds_{date_col}",
                    )
                elif parsed.empty:
                    st.warning("No valid dates are available in the selected field.")
        numeric_filter = st.selectbox(
            "Additional numeric filter", ["No numeric filter"] + numeric,
            key="ex_number_field",
        )
        value_bounds = None
        if numeric_filter != "No numeric filter":
            series = pd.to_numeric(data[numeric_filter], errors="coerce").dropna()
            series = series[series.map(lambda v: bool(pd.notna(v)) and abs(v) != float("inf"))]
            if not series.empty:
                lower_default, upper_default = float(series.min()), float(series.max())
                n1, n2 = st.columns(2)
                with n1:
                    low = st.number_input("Minimum", value=lower_default, key=f"ex_min_{numeric_filter}")
                with n2:
                    high = st.number_input("Maximum", value=upper_default, key=f"ex_max_{numeric_filter}")
                value_bounds = (low, high)

    # A partially selected date range must not silently apply only one endpoint.
    start = end = None
    if date_col != "No date filter" and isinstance(date_bounds, (list, tuple)):
        if len(date_bounds) == 2:
            start, end = date_bounds
        else:
            st.info("Choose both endpoints to apply the date filter.")
    if value_bounds is not None and value_bounds[0] > value_bounds[1]:
        st.error("Minimum must be less than or equal to maximum.")
        return

    try:
        filtered = apply_dashboard_filters(
            data,
            # All selected means unrestricted, including source rows with missing categories.
            category_column=category if selected is not None and len(selected) < len(category_values) else None,
            categories=selected if selected is not None and len(selected) < len(category_values) else None,
            date_column=date_col if start is not None else None,
            start_date=start, end_date=end,
            numeric_column=numeric_filter if value_bounds is not None else None,
            numeric_min=value_bounds[0] if value_bounds is not None else None,
            numeric_max=value_bounds[1] if value_bounds is not None else None,
        ).reset_index(drop=True)
    except (ValueError, TypeError) as error:
        st.error(f"The filter is invalid: {error}")
        return

    st.caption(f"{len(filtered):,} of {len(data):,} records matched the filters.")
    if filtered.empty:
        st.warning("No records match. Adjust the filters to continue.")
        return

    with st.expander("02  ·  Confirm financial field mapping", expanded=True):
        a, b = st.columns(2)
        with a:
            revenue_col = st.selectbox(
                "Gross revenue field", numeric,
                index=_default(numeric, ["Sales", "Gross Revenue", "Revenue"]),
                key="ex_revenue",
            )
            cost_col = st.selectbox(
                "Cost of sales field (optional)", [NONE] + numeric,
                index=_default([NONE] + numeric, ["Total Cost", "COGS", "Cost"]),
                key="ex_cost",
            )
        with b:
            discount_col = st.selectbox("Discounts (optional)", [NONE] + numeric, key="ex_discount")
            returns_col = st.selectbox("Returns (optional)", [NONE] + numeric, key="ex_returns")
        st.caption(
            "Gross revenue must be before separately mapped discounts/returns. "
            "Do not map a field twice or treat Profit as a cost. All amounts must use one currency."
        )

    try:
        calculations, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(
            filtered, revenue_col, cost_col, discount_col, returns_col
        )
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as error:
        st.error(f"Financial calculation blocked: {error}")
        return

    headline = st.columns(4)
    headline[0].metric("Matching Records", f"{len(filtered):,}")
    headline[1].metric("Gross Revenue", f"{totals['gross_revenue']:,.2f}")
    headline[2].metric("Net Revenue", f"{totals['net_revenue']:,.2f}")
    if "profit_after_mapped_costs" in totals:
        headline[3].metric("Profit after mapped costs", f"{totals['profit_after_mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        sub = st.columns(3)
        sub[0].metric("Mapped costs", f"{totals['mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        sub[1].metric("Net business loss", f"{totals['net_business_loss']:,.2f}")
        margin = totals["profit_margin_pct"]
        sub[2].metric("Profit margin", f"{margin:.2f}%" if margin is not None else "Not defined")
        st.caption("Profit shown here is after mapped costs; it is not labelled net profit unless all relevant costs are established.")
    else:
        headline[3].metric("Profit", "Not available")
        st.info("Map a cost-of-sales field to enable profit, loss and margin metrics.")

    chart_col, chart_col2 = st.columns(2)
    with chart_col:
        st.markdown("#### Revenue by category")
        if category != "No category filter" or text_fields:
            dimension = category if category != "No category filter" else text_fields[0]
            chart_frame = pd.DataFrame({
                "Category": filtered[dimension].astype("string").fillna("(Missing)").to_numpy(),
                "Net Revenue": calculations["Net Revenue"].to_numpy(),
            })
            category_summary = (
                chart_frame.groupby("Category", as_index=False, dropna=False)["Net Revenue"]
                .sum().sort_values("Net Revenue", ascending=False).head(12)
            )
            st.plotly_chart(
                px.bar(category_summary, x="Category", y="Net Revenue", title="Top 12 categories"),
                width="stretch",
            )
        else:
            st.info("Add a text/category field to compare revenue across groups.")
    with chart_col2:
        st.markdown("#### Revenue trend")
        if date_col == "No date filter":
            st.info("Select a date field above to display period trends.")
        else:
            cadence = st.selectbox("Period", ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"], key="ex_cadence")
            try:
                time_series, excluded_dates = revenue_by_period(
                    filtered[date_col], calculations["Net Revenue"], cadence
                )
                if excluded_dates:
                    st.warning(f"{excluded_dates:,} rows with invalid/missing dates were excluded from this trend only.")
                if not time_series.empty:
                    st.plotly_chart(
                        px.line(time_series, x="Period", y="Revenue", markers=True,
                                title="Net revenue by period"), width="stretch",
                    )
                    if time_series["Growth %"].notna().any():
                        st.caption("Growth is calculated only for adjacent periods with positive prior-period revenue.")
                else:
                    st.info("No valid dated records remain after filtering.")
            except ValueError as error:
                st.warning(f"Cannot calculate the trend: {error}")

    with st.expander("03  ·  Audit records and download", expanded=False):
        st.dataframe(filtered.head(200), width="stretch")
        output = pd.concat([
            filtered.reset_index(drop=True),
            calculations.reset_index(drop=True).add_prefix("BI · "),
        ], axis=1)
        st.download_button(
            "Download filtered records and financial calculations",
            data=output.to_csv(index=False).encode("utf-8"),
            file_name="python_bi_executive_filtered_data.csv", mime="text/csv", key="ex_export",
        )
        st.caption("Export includes all matching records, not just the 200 previewed rows.")


========== FILE: backup_before_batch56\planning_workspace.py ==========

"""Commercial planning tools with explicit data/assumption boundaries."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import streamlit as st

from business_metrics import break_even_scenario, revenue_by_period


def render_planning_workspace(data: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    st.markdown("### Business Planning & Growth")
    st.caption(
        "Historical growth uses uploaded data. Break-even is a manual scenario; "
        "it is not a prediction of actual future performance."
    )
    growth_tab, model_tab = st.tabs(["Historical revenue growth", "Break-even scenario"])
    with growth_tab:
        numeric = data.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
        dates = [
            col for col in data.columns
            if "date" in str(col).casefold() or "time" in str(col).casefold()
            or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(data[col])
        ]
        if not numeric or not dates or data.empty:
            st.info("Historical growth requires a dataset with a date field and numeric revenue field.")
        else:
            first, second, third = st.columns(3)
            with first:
                date_col = st.selectbox(
                    "Date / timestamp field", dates,
                    index=dates.index("Date") if "Date" in dates else 0,
                    key="plan_date",
                )
            with second:
                revenue_col = st.selectbox(
                    "Revenue field", numeric,
                    index=numeric.index("Sales") if "Sales" in numeric else 0,
                    key="plan_revenue",
                )
            with third:
                period = st.selectbox(
                    "Compare periods", ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"],
                    key="plan_cadence",
                )
            st.caption(
                "Choose a revenue field that is already net of any deductions you want included. "
                "This section does not automatically deduct returns, taxes or discounts."
            )
            try:
                summary, excluded = revenue_by_period(data[date_col], data[revenue_col], period)
                if excluded:
                    st.warning(f"Excluded {excluded:,} rows without valid dates. The source dataset is unchanged.")
                if summary.empty:
                    st.info("There are no valid date/revenue records to summarise.")
                else:
                    cols = st.columns(3)
                    cols[0].metric("Periods observed", f"{len(summary):,}")
                    cols[1].metric("Observed-period revenue", f"{summary['Revenue'].sum():,.2f}")
                    valid_growth = summary["Growth %"].dropna()
                    cols[2].metric(
                        "Latest defined growth",
                        f"{valid_growth.iloc[-1]:+.2f}%" if not valid_growth.empty else "Not available",
                    )
                    st.plotly_chart(
                        px.bar(summary, x="Period", y="Revenue", title=f"Revenue by {period.lower()} period"),
                        width="stretch",
                    )
                    with st.expander("Period-level audit and growth rules"):
                        st.dataframe(summary, width="stretch", hide_index=True)
                        st.caption(
                            "Growth = (current − previous) / previous × 100. Undefined for "
                            "missing intermediate periods or prior revenue of zero/less."
                        )
                        st.download_button(
                            "Export growth analysis", summary.to_csv(index=False).encode("utf-8"),
                            file_name="python_bi_revenue_growth.csv", mime="text/csv", key="plan_growth_export",
                        )
            except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as exc:
                st.warning(f"Revenue growth is unavailable: {exc}")
    with model_tab:
        st.info(
            "Enter values for one consistent currency and unit of production. "
            "These are your scenario assumptions, not inferred amounts from the dataset."
        )
        a, b, c = st.columns(3)
        with a:
            fixed = st.number_input(
                "Fixed costs per planning period", min_value=0.0, value=0.0, step=100.0,
                key="plan_fixed",
            )
        with b:
            price = st.number_input(
                "Selling price per unit", min_value=0.0, value=0.0, step=1.0,
                key="plan_price",
            )
        with c:
            variable = st.number_input(
                "Variable cost per unit", min_value=0.0, value=0.0, step=1.0,
                key="plan_variable",
            )
        if price == 0 and variable == 0 and fixed == 0:
            st.caption("Enter your scenario assumptions above to calculate a break-even point.")
        else:
            try:
                result = break_even_scenario(fixed, price, variable)
                metrics = st.columns(3)
                metrics[0].metric("Unit contribution", f"{result['contribution_per_unit']:,.2f}")
                cm = result["contribution_margin_pct"]
                metrics[1].metric("Contribution margin", f"{cm:.2f}%" if cm is not None else "Not defined")
                units = result["break_even_units"]
                metrics[2].metric("Break-even units", f"{units:,}" if units is not None else "Not attainable")
                if units is None:
                    st.warning("There is no finite break-even volume: unit contribution is zero or negative.")
                else:
                    st.metric("Revenue at break-even units", f"{result['break_even_revenue']:,.2f}")
                st.caption(
                    "Break-even units = ceiling(fixed costs / (unit price − variable cost)). "
                    "The model assumes constant costs and price, and does not include financing, tax or capacity effects."
                )
                st.download_button(
                    "Export scenario assumptions", pd.DataFrame([result]).to_csv(index=False).encode("utf-8"),
                    file_name="python_bi_break_even_scenario.csv", mime="text/csv", key="plan_break_even_export",
                )
            except ValueError as error:
                st.warning(f"Invalid scenario: {error}")

========== FILE: backup_before_batch78\executive_dashboard.py ==========

"""Executive overview: all metrics, trends and exports use the SAME filtered rows."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import streamlit as st

from business_metrics import apply_dashboard_filters, revenue_by_period
from commerce_engine import NONE, calculate_commercial_metrics
from audit_exports import build_financial_export, excel_safe_csv
from performance_insights import category_profitability, loss_making_rows


def _default(options: list[str], preferred: list[str], fallback: int = 0) -> int:
    lower = {name.casefold(): i for i, name in enumerate(options)}
    for name in preferred:
        if name.casefold() in lower:
            return lower[name.casefold()]
    return fallback


def render_executive_dashboard(data: pd.DataFrame, dataset_name: str) -> None:
    st.header("Executive Performance Dashboard")
    st.caption(f"Dataset: {dataset_name}  •  All figures on this page reflect the selected filters.")
    if data.empty:
        st.warning("The active dataset has no records.")
        return

    numeric = data.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
    if not numeric:
        st.warning("No numeric financial fields are available. Prepare the source data first.")
        return

    text_fields = data.select_dtypes(include=["object", "string", "category"]).columns.tolist()
    category_options = ["No category filter"] + text_fields
    date_options = ["No date filter"] + [
        col for col in data.columns
        if "date" in str(col).casefold() or "time" in str(col).casefold()
        or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(data[col])
    ]

    st.markdown("### Business performance")
    with st.expander("01  ·  Refine dataset", expanded=True):
        first, second = st.columns(2)
        with first:
            category = st.selectbox(
                "Category", category_options,
                index=_default(category_options, ["Product", "Region", "City"]),
                key="ex_category",
            )
            selected = None
            category_values = []
            if category != "No category filter":
                category_values = sorted(data[category].dropna().astype(str).unique().tolist())
                if len(category_values) > 250:
                    st.info("This field has over 250 values; use another category for an interactive selector.")
                    category = "No category filter"
                else:
                    selected = st.multiselect(
                        "Values to include", category_values, default=category_values,
                        key=f"ex_values_{category}",
                    )
        with second:
            date_col = st.selectbox(
                "Date field", date_options,
                index=_default(date_options, ["Date", "Order Date", "Transaction Date"]),
                key="ex_date_field",
            )
            date_bounds = None
            if date_col != "No date filter":
                apply_date_range = st.checkbox(
                    "Restrict records to a date range", value=False, key="ex_use_date_filter"
                )
                parsed = pd.to_datetime(data[date_col], errors="coerce", format="mixed").dropna()
                if not parsed.empty and apply_date_range:
                    date_bounds = st.date_input(
                        "Date range", value=(parsed.min().date(), parsed.max().date()),
                        min_value=parsed.min().date(), max_value=parsed.max().date(),
                        key=f"ex_date_bounds_{date_col}",
                    )
                elif parsed.empty:
                    st.warning("No valid dates are available in the selected field.")
        numeric_filter = st.selectbox(
            "Additional numeric filter", ["No numeric filter"] + numeric,
            key="ex_number_field",
        )
        value_bounds = None
        if numeric_filter != "No numeric filter":
            series = pd.to_numeric(data[numeric_filter], errors="coerce").dropna()
            series = series[series.map(lambda v: bool(pd.notna(v)) and abs(v) != float("inf"))]
            if not series.empty:
                lower_default, upper_default = float(series.min()), float(series.max())
                n1, n2 = st.columns(2)
                with n1:
                    low = st.number_input("Minimum", value=lower_default, key=f"ex_min_{numeric_filter}")
                with n2:
                    high = st.number_input("Maximum", value=upper_default, key=f"ex_max_{numeric_filter}")
                value_bounds = (low, high)

    # A partially selected date range must not silently apply only one endpoint.
    start = end = None
    if date_col != "No date filter" and isinstance(date_bounds, (list, tuple)):
        if len(date_bounds) == 2:
            start, end = date_bounds
        else:
            st.info("Choose both endpoints to apply the date filter.")
    if value_bounds is not None and value_bounds[0] > value_bounds[1]:
        st.error("Minimum must be less than or equal to maximum.")
        return

    try:
        filtered = apply_dashboard_filters(
            data,
            # All selected means unrestricted, including source rows with missing categories.
            category_column=category if selected is not None and len(selected) < len(category_values) else None,
            categories=selected if selected is not None and len(selected) < len(category_values) else None,
            date_column=date_col if start is not None else None,
            start_date=start, end_date=end,
            numeric_column=numeric_filter if value_bounds is not None else None,
            numeric_min=value_bounds[0] if value_bounds is not None else None,
            numeric_max=value_bounds[1] if value_bounds is not None else None,
        ).reset_index(drop=True)
    except (ValueError, TypeError) as error:
        st.error(f"The filter is invalid: {error}")
        return

    st.caption(f"{len(filtered):,} of {len(data):,} records matched the filters.")
    if filtered.empty:
        st.warning("No records match. Adjust the filters to continue.")
        return

    with st.expander("02  ·  Confirm financial field mapping", expanded=True):
        a, b = st.columns(2)
        with a:
            revenue_col = st.selectbox(
                "Gross revenue field", numeric,
                index=_default(numeric, ["Sales", "Gross Revenue", "Revenue"]),
                key="ex_revenue",
            )
            cost_col = st.selectbox(
                "Cost of sales field (optional)", [NONE] + numeric,
                index=_default([NONE] + numeric, ["Total Cost", "COGS", "Cost"]),
                key="ex_cost",
            )
        with b:
            discount_col = st.selectbox("Discounts (optional)", [NONE] + numeric, key="ex_discount")
            returns_col = st.selectbox("Returns (optional)", [NONE] + numeric, key="ex_returns")
        st.caption(
            "Gross revenue must be before separately mapped discounts/returns. "
            "Do not map a field twice or treat Profit as a cost. All amounts must use one currency."
        )

    try:
        calculations, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(
            filtered, revenue_col, cost_col, discount_col, returns_col
        )
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as error:
        st.error(f"Financial calculation blocked: {error}")
        return

    headline = st.columns(4)
    headline[0].metric("Matching Records", f"{len(filtered):,}")
    headline[1].metric("Gross Revenue", f"{totals['gross_revenue']:,.2f}")
    headline[2].metric("Net Revenue", f"{totals['net_revenue']:,.2f}")
    if "profit_after_mapped_costs" in totals:
        headline[3].metric("Profit after mapped costs", f"{totals['profit_after_mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        sub = st.columns(3)
        sub[0].metric("Mapped costs", f"{totals['mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        sub[1].metric("Net business loss", f"{totals['net_business_loss']:,.2f}")
        margin = totals["profit_margin_pct"]
        sub[2].metric("Profit margin", f"{margin:.2f}%" if margin is not None else "Not defined")
        st.caption("Profit shown here is after mapped costs; it is not labelled net profit unless all relevant costs are established.")
    else:
        headline[3].metric("Profit", "Not available")
        st.info("Map a cost-of-sales field to enable profit, loss and margin metrics.")

    chart_col, chart_col2 = st.columns(2)
    with chart_col:
        st.markdown("#### Revenue by category")
        if category != "No category filter" or text_fields:
            dimension = category if category != "No category filter" else text_fields[0]
            chart_frame = pd.DataFrame({
                "Category": filtered[dimension].astype("string").fillna("(Missing)").to_numpy(),
                "Net Revenue": calculations["Net Revenue"].to_numpy(),
            })
            category_summary = (
                chart_frame.groupby("Category", as_index=False, dropna=False)["Net Revenue"]
                .sum().sort_values("Net Revenue", ascending=False).head(12)
            )
            st.plotly_chart(
                px.bar(category_summary, x="Category", y="Net Revenue", title="Top 12 categories"),
                width="stretch",
            )
        else:
            st.info("Add a text/category field to compare revenue across groups.")
    with chart_col2:
        st.markdown("#### Revenue trend")
        if date_col == "No date filter":
            st.info("Select a date field above to display period trends.")
        else:
            cadence = st.selectbox("Period", ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"], key="ex_cadence")
            try:
                time_series, excluded_dates = revenue_by_period(
                    filtered[date_col], calculations["Net Revenue"], cadence
                )
                if excluded_dates:
                    st.warning(f"{excluded_dates:,} rows with invalid/missing dates were excluded from this trend only.")
                if not time_series.empty:
                    st.plotly_chart(
                        px.line(time_series, x="Period", y="Revenue", markers=True,
                                title="Net revenue by period"), width="stretch",
                    )
                    if time_series["Growth %"].notna().any():
                        st.caption("Growth is calculated only for adjacent periods with positive prior-period revenue.")
                else:
                    st.info("No valid dated records remain after filtering.")
            except ValueError as error:
                st.warning(f"Cannot calculate the trend: {error}")

    with st.expander("03  ·  Audit records and download", expanded=False):
        st.dataframe(filtered.head(200), width="stretch")
        output = build_financial_export(filtered, calculations)
        st.download_button(
            "Download filtered records and financial calculations",
            data=excel_safe_csv(output),
            file_name="python_bi_executive_filtered_data.csv", mime="text/csv", key="ex_export",
        )
        st.caption(
            "Export includes all matching records, not just the 200 previewed rows. "
            "Calculated fields are named BI_Gross_Revenue, BI_Net_Revenue, etc. "
            "Spreadsheet-dangerous text is protected in the exported copy."
        )

    if "Mapped Costs" in calculations.columns:
        with st.expander("04  ·  Category profitability and transaction exceptions", expanded=False):
            st.caption(
                "These results use exactly the filtered records and financial field mapping above. "
                "Profit is after mapped costs; it is not necessarily accounting net profit."
            )
            available_categories = [
                c for c in filtered.columns
                if pd.api.types.is_object_dtype(filtered[c])
                or pd.api.types.is_string_dtype(filtered[c])
                or isinstance(filtered[c].dtype, pd.CategoricalDtype)
            ]
            if available_categories:
                category_field = st.selectbox(
                    "Group profitability by", available_categories,
                    index=_default(available_categories, ["Product", "Region", "City"]),
                    key="ex_profit_category",
                )
                groups = category_profitability(filtered, calculations, category_field)
                st.dataframe(groups.head(100), width="stretch", hide_index=True)
                st.download_button(
                    "Export category profitability",
                    data=excel_safe_csv(groups),
                    file_name="python_bi_category_profitability.csv",
                    mime="text/csv", key="ex_profitability_export",
                )
                st.caption(
                    "Category totals reconcile to the filtered net revenue, mapped costs "
                    "and aggregate profit above. A zero net-revenue category has undefined margin."
                )
            else:
                st.info("No categorical source field is available for grouped analysis.")
            exceptions = loss_making_rows(filtered, calculations)
            st.metric("Loss-making transactions", f"{len(exceptions):,}")
            if not exceptions.empty:
                st.dataframe(exceptions.head(100), width="stretch")
                st.download_button(
                    "Export loss-making transactions",
                    data=excel_safe_csv(exceptions),
                    file_name="python_bi_loss_making_transactions.csv",
                    mime="text/csv", key="ex_loss_export",
                )
            else:
                st.success("No transactions have a negative profit after mapped costs.")



FOLDER: backup_before_batch910

========== FILE: backup_before_batch910\executive_dashboard.py ==========

"""Executive overview: all metrics, trends and exports use the SAME filtered rows."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import streamlit as st

from commerce_engine import NONE, calculate_commercial_metrics
from audit_exports import build_financial_export, excel_safe_csv
from performance_insights import category_profitability, loss_making_rows
from executive_addons import render_comparison_panel, render_readiness_panel


def _default(options: list[str], preferred: list[str], fallback: int = 0) -> int:

    for name in preferred:
        if name.casefold() in lower:
            return lower[name.casefold()]
    return fallback

        or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(data[col])
    ]

            )

                category_values = sorted(data[category].dropna().astype(str).unique().tolist())
                if len(category_values) > 250:
                    st.info("This field has over 250 values; use another category for an interactive selector.")
                    category = "No category filter"
                else:
                    selected = st.multiselect(
                        "Values to include", category_values, default=category_values,
                        key=f"ex_values_{category}",
                    )
        with second:
            date_col = st.selectbox(
                "Date field", date_options,
